Key Requirements Pulled Forward

  • Show/movie/episode cards remain shared component families, with content-specific metadata hierarchy and shared action row.
  • Episode card contexts include dashboard schedule/history, calendar, Watch Me rows, and show popup episode carousel.
  • Old image-overlay availability badge behavior is explicitly superseded by the current no-overlay/popcorn-availability contract.
  • Calendar keeps wall-calendar semantics, full-width calendar page rules, sticky controls/weekday row, +X more/Show less behavior, and no sidebar.
  • Show popup requirements include Exit-first focus, two-column hero, key/value facts, continuous season band, horizontal season selector, jump controls, and episode carousel.
  • Movie popup requirements include sticky header, hero with poster/metadata, compact fact grid, overview, backdrop, external links, and predictable TV D-pad order.
  • Focus navigation requirements include one global arrow-key system, top-layer popup trapping, background scroll lock, spatial D-pad movement, and no hidden/offscreen focus targets.
  • TMDB field inventory is preserved as required schema coverage input for identifier/title/date/rating/overview/image/genre/production/recommendation fields.
  • Icon asset guidance preserves SVG as preferred custom icon format and transparency requirements for raster icons.
